## lib/models/brand_config.dart

Provenance: scanner (filesystem metadata and bounded content acquisition)

````
# File: lib/models/brand_config.dart

## Changes (+101/-0)

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,101 @@
+enum BrandId {
+  snagUk('snag_uk'),
+  punchListUs('punch_list_us'),
+  defectGlobal('defect_global');
+
+  final String id;
+  const BrandId(this.id);
+
+  static BrandId fromString(String? raw) {
+    if (raw == null) return BrandId.snagUk;
+    final clean = raw.toLowerCase().trim();
+    if (clean.contains('punch') || clean.contains('us')) {
+      return BrandId.punchListUs;
+    }
+    if (clean.contains('defect') || clean.contains('global') || clean.contains('row')) {
+      return BrandId.defectGlobal;
+    }
+    return BrandId.snagUk;
+  }
+}
+
+class BrandConfig {
+  final BrandId brandId;
+  final String brandName;
+  final String domain;
+  final String defectTerm;        // "Snag", "Punch Item", "Defect"
+  final String defectTermPlural;  // "Snags", "Punch Items", "Defects"
+  final String reportTerm;        // "Snagging Report", "Punch List Report", "Defect Report"
+  final String supportEmail;
+  final String logoAssetPath;
+
+  const BrandConfig({
+    required this.brandId,
+    required this.brandName,
+    required this.domain,
+    required this.defectTerm,
+    required this.defectTermPlural,
+    required this.reportTerm,
+    required this.supportEmail,
+    this.logoAssetPath = 'assets/icons/logo.svg',
+  });
+
+  /// UK Regional Brand (Snag Ninja)
+  static const snagUk = BrandConfig(
+    brandId: BrandId.snagUk,
+    brandName: 'Snag Ninja',
+    domain: 'snagninja.com',
+    defectTerm: 'Snag',
+    defectTermPlural: 'Snags',
+    reportTerm: 'Snagging Report',
+    supportEmail: 'support@snagninja.com',
+  );
+
+  /// US Regional Brand (Punch List Ninja)
+  static const punchListUs = BrandConfig(
+    brandId: BrandId.punchListUs,
+    brandName: 'Punch List Ninja',
+    domain: 'punchlistninja.com',
+    defectTerm: 'Punch Item',
+    defectTermPlural: 'Punch Items',
+    reportTerm: 'Punch List Report',
+    supportEmail: 'support@punchlistninja.com',
+  );
+
+  /// Global / Rest of World Brand (Defect Ninja)
+  static const defectGlobal = BrandConfig(
+    brandId: BrandId.defectGlobal,
+    brandName: 'Defect Ninja',
+    domain: 'defectninja.com',
+    defectTerm: 'Defect',
+    defectTermPlural: 'Defects',
+    reportTerm: 'Defect Report',
+    supportEmail: 'support@defectninja.com',
+  );
+
+  static BrandConfig fromBrandId(BrandId id) {
+    switch (id) {
+      case BrandId.punchListUs:
+        return punchListUs;
+      case BrandId.defectGlobal:
+        return defectGlobal;
+      case BrandId.snagUk:
+        return snagUk;
+    }
+  }
+
+  static BrandConfig fromString(String? raw) {
+    return fromBrandId(BrandId.fromString(raw));
+  }
+
+  /// Construct dynamic web URL for in-app links or workspace sharing
+  String getUrl(String path) {
+    final cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : '/$path';
+    return 'https://$domain$cleanPath';
+  }
+
+  /// Web workspace email link for store-compliant PDF export flows
+  String getReportUnlockUrl(String reportId) {
+    return 'https://$domain/reports/$reportId/unlock';
+  }
+}
````

````
+  void _setupRealtime() {
+    final user = _supabase.client.auth.currentUser;

+    if (_subscription != null) return;
+
+    debugPrint('Setting up real-time for reports & report_items for: ${user.email}');
+    _subscription = _supabase.client
+        .channel('reports_realtime_${user.id}')
+        .onPostgresChanges(
+          event: PostgresChangeEvent.all,
+          schema: 'public',
+          table: 'reports',
+          callback: (payload) {
+            debugPrint('Real-time update on reports: ${payload.eventType}');
+            refreshReports();
+          },
+        )
+        .onPostgresChanges(
+          event: PostgresChangeEvent.all,
+          schema: 'public',
+          table: 'report_items',
+          callback: (payload) {
+            debugPrint('Real-time update on report_items: ${payload.eventType}');
+            refreshReports();
+          },
+        )
+        .subscribe();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    if (_subscription != null) {
+      _supabase.client.removeChannel(_subscription!);
+    }
+    super.dispose();
   }

+  Future<void> updateReportItemStatus({
+    required String reportId,
+    required String itemId,
+    required String newStatus,
+    String? builderComment,
+    String? builderImagePath,
+  }) async {
+    final now = DateTime.now();
+    state = state.map((report) {
+      if (report.id == reportId) {
+        final updatedItems = report.items.map((item) {
+          if (item.id == itemId) {
+            return item.copyWith(
+              status: newStatus,
+              builderComment: builderComment,
+              builderImagePath: builderImagePath,
+            );
+          }
+          return item;
+        }).toList();
+
+        final calculatedOverallStatus = calculateReportStatus(updatedItems);
+        final newResolvedCount = calculateResolvedCount(updatedItems);
+
+        return report.copyWith(
+          status: calculatedOverallStatus,
+          resolvedCount: newResolvedCount,
+          items: updatedItems,
+          updatedAt: now,
+        );
+      }
+      return report;
+    }).toList();
+    _persistence.saveReports(state);
+
+    // Also update the overall report status + resolved_count in Supabase
+    final report = state.cast<Report?>().firstWhere(
+      (r) => r?.id == reportId,
+      orElse: () => null,
+    );
+    if (report == null) return; // Guard: report not found in state
+    
+    await _supabase.updateReportItem(itemId, {
+      'status': newStatus,
+      'builder_comment': builderComment,
+      'builder_image_path': builderImagePath,
+    });
+    await _supabase.updateReport(reportId, {
+      'status': report.status,
+      'resolved_count': report.resolvedCount,
+      'updated_at': now.toIso8601String(),
+    });
+  }
+
+  Future<void> addItemToReport(String reportId, ReportItem newItem) async {
+    final now = DateTime.now();
+    state = state.map((report) {
+      if (report.id == reportId) {
+        final updatedItems = [...report.items, newItem];
+        // If report was Pending, Draft, or For Review, move it back to Progress (Active) since new issues were added
+        String newStatus = report.status;
+        final s = report.status.toLowerCase();
+        if (s == 'pending' || s == 'draft' || s == 'for review' || s == 'forreview') {
+          newStatus = 'Progress';
+        }
+        
+        return report.copyWith(
+          items: updatedItems,
+          itemsCount: report.itemsCount + 1,
+          status: newStatus,
+          updatedAt: now,
+        );
+      }
+      return report;
+    }).toList();
+    _persistence.saveReports(state);
+

+      'image_paths': newItem.imagePaths,

+    });
+    
+    // Update the report's item count, status, resolved_count, and updated_at in Supabase
+    final report = state.cast<Report?>().firstWhere(

+    );
+    if (report == null) return;
+    await _supabase.updateReport(reportId, {

+  /// Instantly updates a single item in the local state without fetching from Supabase.
+  /// Used to reflect sign-off changes immediately on the UI.
+  void updateItemLocally({
+    required String itemId,
+    required String newStatus,
+    String? builderComment,
+    String? builderImagePath,
+  }) {
+    final updatedItems = state.items.map((item) {
+      if (item.id == itemId) {
+        return item.copyWith(
+          status: newStatus,
+          builderComment: builderComment,
+          builderImagePath: builderImagePath,
+        );
+      }
+      return item;
+    }).toList();
+    final newResolvedCount = calculateResolvedCount(updatedItems);
+    final updatedReport = state.report?.copyWith(resolvedCount: newResolvedCount);
+    state = state.copyWith(
+      items: updatedItems,
+      report: updatedReport,
+    );
+  }
+
+  void addItemLocally(ReportItem item) {
+    state = state.copyWith(
+      items: [...state.items, item],
+    );
+  }
 }

+        customers.add(Customer(

+
+      state = customers;
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error refreshing my customers: $e');
+    }
+  }

+      debugPrint('Error cleaning up customer invitations: $e');

+
+    await refresh();
+  }
+}
````

````
   void initState() {
     super.initState();
     _tabController = TabController(length: 2, vsync: this);
+    // Refresh invitations on screen load
+    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
+      ref
+          .read(receivedConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier)
+          .fetchInvitations();
+      ref.read(reportInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+      ref.read(sentConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+      ref.read(sentReportInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+
+      // Honour any tab index set by notification tap
+      final targetTab = ref.read(inviteTabProvider);
+      if (targetTab >= 0 && targetTab < 2) {
+        _tabController.animateTo(targetTab);
+        // Reset so it doesn't re-apply on next open
+        ref.read(inviteTabProvider.notifier).state = 0;
+      }
+    });
+
+    // Refresh sent invitations every time the Sent tab is opened and clear Sent tab badge
+    _tabController.addListener(() {
+      if (_tabController.index == 1 && !_tabController.indexIsChanging) {
+        ref.read(unseenSentInvitesBadgeProvider.notifier).state = false;
+        ref.read(sentConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+        ref.read(sentReportInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+      }
+    });
   }

+
+  Color _getStatusColor(String? status) {
+    final s = status?.toLowerCase();
+    switch (s) {
+      case 'accepted':
+        return Colors.green;
+      case 'declined':
+        return Colors.red;
+      case 'pending':
+      default:
+        return Colors.orange[800]!;
+    }
+  }
 }
````

````
+                  .where((inv) => !isInviteExpired24h(inv))
+                  .toList();
+
+              return RefreshIndicator(
+                onRefresh: () async {
+                  await Future.wait([
+                    ref
+                        .read(receivedConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier)
+                        .fetchInvitations(),
+                    ref
+                        .read(sentConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier)
+                        .fetchInvitations(),
+                    ref
+                        .read(sentReportInvitationsProvider.notifier)
+                        .fetchInvitations(),
+                  ]);
+                },
+                child:
+                    _inviteFilter == 'Received'
+                        ? (receivedInvites.isEmpty
+                            ? _buildEmptyTab('No received invites')
+                            : ListView(
+                              physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
+                              padding: AppSizes.screenPadding.copyWith(
+                                bottom: 80,
+                              ),
+                              children:
+                                  receivedInvites
+                                      .map(
+                                        (inv) => _buildReceivedInviteCard(
+                                          context,
+                                          ref,
+                                          inv,
+                                          theme,
+                                        ),
+                                      )
+                                      .toList(),
+                            ))
+                        : (allSentInvites.isEmpty
+                            ? _buildEmptyTab('No sent invites')
+                            : ListView(
+                              physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
+                              padding: AppSizes.screenPadding.copyWith(
+                                bottom: 80,
+                              ),
+                              children:
+                                  allSentInvites
+                                      .map((inv) => _buildSentInviteCard(inv))
+                                      .toList(),
+                            )),
+              );
+            },
+          ),
+        ),
+      ],
+    );
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildFilterChip(String label) {
+    final isSelected = _inviteFilter == label;
+    return GestureDetector(
+      onTap: () {
+        setState(() => _inviteFilter = label);
+        // Refresh the appropriate data when switching chips
+        if (label == 'Sent') {
+          ref.read(sentConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+          ref.read(sentReportInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+        } else {
+          ref.read(receivedConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+        }
+      },
+      child: Container(
+        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
+        decoration: BoxDecoration(
+          color: isSelected ? AppColors.primaryColor : Colors.grey[200],
+          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
+        ),
+        child: Text(
+          label,
+          style: TextStyle(
+            color: isSelected ? Colors.white : Colors.grey[700],
+            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+          ),
+        ),
       ),
     );
   }
 
+  Widget _buildEmptyTab(String message) {
+    return ListView(
+      physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
+      children: [
+        SizedBox(height: AppSizes.screenHeight * 0.2),
+        Center(child: Text(message, style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey[600]))),
+      ],
+    );
+  }
+
   void _showAddBuilderDialog(BuildContext context) async {
     await Navigator.push(
       context,
       MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const InviteBuildersScreen()),
     );
   }
 
-  Widget _buildEmptyState(AppTheme theme) {

-            fontSize: AppSizes.bodyText,
-            color: Colors.grey[600],
+  Widget _buildReceivedInviteCard(
+    BuildContext context,
+    WidgetRef ref,
+    Map<String, dynamic> inv,
+    AppTheme theme,
+  ) {
+    final builder = inv['builder'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
+    final fName =
+        builder?['first_name']?.toString() ?? inv['builder_name'] ?? '';
+    final lName = builder?['last_name']?.toString() ?? '';
+    final name =
+        '$fName $lName'.trim().isEmpty ? 'A Builder' : '$fName $lName'.trim();
+    final email = builder?['email']?.toString() ?? inv['builder_email'] ?? '';
+
+    return Container(
+      margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSizes.smallSpace),
+      decoration: BoxDecoration(
+        color: Colors.white,
+        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppSizes.containerRadius),
+        border: Border.all(
+          color: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.3),
+          width: 1.5,
+        ),
+        boxShadow: [
+          BoxShadow(
+            color: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
+            blurRadius: 4,
+            offset: const Offset(0, 2),
           ),
+        ],
+      ),
+      child: ListTile(
+        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.all(AppSizes.smallSpace),
+        leading: CircleAvatar(
+          backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
+          child: Icon(Icons.handshake_outlined, color: AppColors.primaryColor),
         ),
+        title: Text(name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
+        subtitle: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Text(email, style: TextStyle(fontSize: AppSizes.bodyText * 0.9)),
+            const SizedBox(height: 4),
+            Text(
+              'Wants to be your builder',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                color: AppColors.primaryColor,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
+                fontSize: AppSizes.bodyText * 0.8,
+              ),
+            ),
+          ],
+        ),
+        trailing: Row(
+          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+          children: [
+            IconButton(
+              icon: const Icon(Icons.check_circle, color: Colors.green),
+              onPressed:
+                  () => ref
+                      .read(receivedConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier)
+                      .respond(inv['id'].toString(), true),
+            ),
+            IconButton(
+              icon: const Icon(Icons.cancel, color: Colors.red),
+              onPressed:
+                  () => ref
+                      .read(receivedConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier)
+                      .respond(inv['id'].toString(), false),
+            ),
+          ],
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildSentInviteCard(Map<String, dynamic> inv) {
+    final isReportInvite = inv.containsKey('report_id');
+    final builderProfile = inv['builder'] as Map<String, dynamic>?;
+
+    String name = inv['builder_name'] ?? '';
+    String email = inv['builder_email'] ?? '';
+
+    if (builderProfile != null) {
+      final fName = builderProfile['first_name']?.toString() ?? '';
+      final lName = builderProfile['last_name']?.toString() ?? '';
+      final profileName = '$fName $lName'.trim();
+      if (profileName.isNotEmpty) name = profileName;
+      final profileEmail = builderProfile['email']?.toString() ?? '';
+      if (profileEmail.isNotEmpty) email = profileEmail;
+    }
+
+    if (name.isEmpty) name = 'A Builder';
+
+    String? reportInfo;
+    if (isReportInvite && inv['reports'] != null) {
+      final report = inv['reports'] as Map<String, dynamic>;
+      final title = report['title'] ?? 'Report';
+      reportInfo = 'Project: $title';
+    }
+
+    final status = inv['status']?.toString() ?? 'Pending';
+    final statusColor = _getInviteStatusColor(status);
+
+    return Container(
+      margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSizes.smallSpace),
+      decoration: BoxDecoration(
+        color: Colors.white,
+        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppSizes.containerRadius),
+        border: Border.all(color: Colors.grey[300]!),
       ),
+      child: ListTile(
+        leading: CircleAvatar(
+          backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
+          child: Text(
+            name.isNotEmpty ? name[0].toUpperCase() : 'B',
+            style: const TextStyle(
+              color: AppColors.primaryColor,
+              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+            ),
+          ),
+        ),
+        title: Text(name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
+        subtitle: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Text(email),
+            if (reportInfo != null)
+              Text(
+                reportInfo,
+                style: const TextStyle(
+                  fontSize: 10,
+                  fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
+                  color: Colors.blueGrey,
+                ),
+              ),
+          ],
+        ),
+        trailing: Column(
+          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
+          children: [
+            Container(
+              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
+              decoration: BoxDecoration(
+                color: statusColor.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
+                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
+              ),
+              child: Text(
+                status.toLowerCase() == 'pending' ? 'Pending Invite' : status,
+                style: TextStyle(
+                  color: statusColor,
+                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                  fontSize: 10,
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+            if (status.toLowerCase() == 'accepted' || status.toLowerCase() == 'declined') ...[
+              const SizedBox(height: 2),
+              Builder(
+                builder: (context) {
+                  final dateStr = inv['updated_at'] ?? inv['created_at'];
+                  if (dateStr == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
+                  final updatedAt = DateTime.tryParse(dateStr.toString())?.toLocal();
+                  if (updatedAt == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
+                  final hoursLeft = (24 - DateTime.now().difference(updatedAt).inHours).clamp(1, 24);
+                  return Text(
+                    'Auto-clears in ${hoursLeft}h',
+                    style: TextStyle(
+                      fontSize: 9,
+                      color: Colors.grey[500],
+                      fontStyle: FontStyle.italic,
+                    ),
+                  );
+                },
+              ),
+            ],
+          ],
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  Color _getInviteStatusColor(String status) {
+    final s = status.toLowerCase();
+    if (s == 'accepted') return Colors.green;
+    if (s == 'declined') return Colors.red;
+    return Colors.orange;
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildEmptyState(AppTheme theme) {
+    return ListView(
+      physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
+      children: [
+        SizedBox(height: AppSizes.screenHeight * 0.3),
+        Center(
+          child: Padding(
+            padding: AppSizes.screenPadding,
+            child: Text(
+              'No builders yet. Tap + to invite or add a builder.',
+              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.bodyText,
+                color: Colors.grey[600],
+              ),
+            ),
+          ),
+        ),
+      ],
+    );
+  }
+
+  void _showRemoveConfirmation(
+    BuildContext context,
+    WidgetRef ref,
+    BuilderProfile builder,
+  ) {
+    showDialog(
+      context: context,
+      builder:
+          (context) => AlertDialog(
+            title: const Text('Remove Builder'),
+            content: Text('Are you sure you want to remove ${builder.name}?'),
+            actions: [
+              TextButton(
+                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
+                child: const Text('Cancel'),
+              ),
+              TextButton(
+                onPressed: () async {
+                  Navigator.pop(context);
+                  await ref
+                      .read(myBuildersProvider.notifier)
+                      .removeBuilder(builder.id);
+                },
+                style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: Colors.red),
+                child: const Text('Remove'),
+              ),
+            ],
+          ),
     );
   }
 }
````

````
+    LocalNotificationService.onNotificationTap = (payload) async {
+      if (!mounted) return;
+      final accountType = ref.read(authProvider).accountType;
+      final isBuilder = accountType == 'Builder';
+
+      if (payload == null) {
+        // No payload — just go to reports/home
+        ref.read(navProvider.notifier).state =
+            isBuilder ? NavItem.assignedReports : NavItem.reports;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      // ── Invite: received ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+      if (payload == 'invite:received' || payload == 'invitation' ||
+          payload.contains('invite')) {
+        ref.read(navProvider.notifier).state =
+            isBuilder ? NavItem.invites : NavItem.builders;
+        ref.read(receivedConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+        ref.read(reportInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+        // Signal to switch to Received tab (index 0) — picked up by BuilderInvitesContentScreen
+        ref.read(inviteTabProvider.notifier).state = 0;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      // ── Invite: sent (response notification — accepted/declined) ──────────
+      if (payload == 'invite:sent') {
+        ref.read(navProvider.notifier).state =
+            isBuilder ? NavItem.invites : NavItem.builders;
+        ref.read(sentConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+        // Signal to switch to Sent tab (index 1)
+        ref.read(inviteTabProvider.notifier).state = 1;
+        return;
+      }
+
+      // ── Report: completed ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+      if (payload.startsWith('report:completed')) {
+        ref.read(navProvider.notifier).state =
+            isBuilder ? NavItem.assignedReports : NavItem.reports;
+        ref.read(notificationsProvider.notifier).markAllAsRead();
+        // Set report tab to Completed (index 2 for builder, 1 for customer)
+        ref.read(reportTabProvider.notifier).state = isBuilder ? 2 : 1;
+        await ref.read(isBuilder
+            ? assignedReportsProvider.notifier
+            : reportsProvider.notifier).refreshReports();
+
+        // Try to open specific report detail
+        final parts = payload.split(':');
+        if (parts.length >= 3) {
+          final reportId = parts.sublist(2).join(':').trim();
+          final reports = ref.read(isBuilder ? assignedReportsProvider : reportsProvider);
+          final match = reports.cast<Report?>().firstWhere(
+            (r) => r?.id == reportId,
+            orElse: () => null,
+          );
+          if (match != null && mounted) {
+            ref.read(selectedReportProvider.notifier).state = match;
+            Navigator.push(
+              context,
+              MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const ReportDetailsScreen()),
+            );
+          }
+        }
+        return;
+      }
+
+      // ── Report: specific ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+      if (payload.startsWith('report:')) {
+        final reportId = payload.replaceFirst('report:', '').trim();
+        ref.read(navProvider.notifier).state =
+            isBuilder ? NavItem.assignedReports : NavItem.reports;
+        ref.read(notificationsProvider.notifier).markAllAsRead();
+        await ref.read(isBuilder
+            ? assignedReportsProvider.notifier
+            : reportsProvider.notifier).refreshReports();
+
+        final reports = ref.read(isBuilder ? assignedReportsProvider : reportsProvider);
+        final match = reports.cast<Report?>().firstWhere(
+          (r) => r?.id == reportId,
+          orElse: () => null,
+        );
+        if (match != null && mounted) {
+          ref.read(selectedReportProvider.notifier).state = match;
+          Navigator.push(
+            context,
+            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const ReportDetailsScreen()),
+          );
+        }
+        return;
+      }
+
+      // ── Fallback: reports page ────────────────────────────────────────────
+      ref.read(navProvider.notifier).state =
+          isBuilder ? NavItem.assignedReports : NavItem.reports;
+    };
+  }
````

````
# File: lib/screens/invite_customers_screen.dart

## Changes (+299/-0)

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,299 @@
+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/providers/app_providers.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/services/supabase_service.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/colors.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/styles.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/sizes.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/widgets/custom_textfield.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/widgets/custom_snackbar.dart';
+
+class InviteCustomersScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
+  const InviteCustomersScreen({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  ConsumerState<InviteCustomersScreen> createState() =>
+      _InviteCustomersScreenState();
+}
+
+class _InviteCustomersScreenState extends ConsumerState<InviteCustomersScreen> {
+  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
+  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
+  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
+  bool _isInviting = false;
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _nameController.dispose();
+    _emailController.dispose();
+    _phoneController.dispose();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  Future<void> _handleInvite() async {
+    if (_nameController.text.trim().isEmpty) {
+      CustomSnackBar.showWarning(
+        context: context,
+        message: 'Please enter customer name',
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+    if (!_emailController.text.trim().contains('@')) {
+      CustomSnackBar.showWarning(
+        context: context,
+        message: 'Please enter a valid email address',
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+
+    setState(() {
+      _isInviting = true;
+    });
+
+    try {
+      final email = _emailController.text.trim().toLowerCase();
+      final supabase = Supabase.instance.client;
+      final currentUser = supabase.auth.currentUser;
+
+      if (currentUser == null) {
+        throw Exception('User not logged in');
+      }
+
+      // Check if customer exists in user_profiles
+      final existingUser = await supabase
+          .from('user_profiles')
+          .select('id, account_type')
+          .eq('email', email)
+          .maybeSingle();
+      
+      final bool alreadyExists = existingUser != null;
+
+      // Check if already connected in user_builder_assignments
+      if (alreadyExists) {
+        final connection = await supabase
+            .from('user_builder_assignments')
+            .select('id, active_for_builder')
+            .eq('builder_id', currentUser.id)
+            .eq('customer_id', existingUser['id'])
+            .neq('active_for_builder', false)  // Only block if still actively connected
+            .maybeSingle();
+
+        if (connection != null) {
+          if (mounted) {
+            setState(() {
+              _isInviting = false;
+            });
+            CustomSnackBar.showInfo(
+              context: context,
+              message: 'This customer is already added into your My Customer list',
+            );
+          }
+          return;
+        }
+      }
+
+      // Send the connection invitation
+      final success = await ref
+          .read(supabaseServiceProvider)
+          .sendCustomerConnectionInvitation(
+            customerEmail: email,
+            customerName: _nameController.text.trim(),
+          );
+
+      if (success) {
+        final senderFirstName = currentUser.userMetadata?['first_name'] ?? '';
+        final senderLastName = currentUser.userMetadata?['last_name'] ?? '';
+        final senderDisplayName = '$senderFirstName $senderLastName'.trim();
+        // Format: "Name (Builder)" so the email reads naturally
+        final senderWithRole =
+            '${senderDisplayName.isNotEmpty ? senderDisplayName : 'A Builder'} (Builder)';
+
+        try {
+          await supabase.functions.invoke(
+            'send-invite-email',
+            body: {
+              'to': email,
+              'sender_name': senderWithRole,
+              'exists': alreadyExists,
+              'role': 'Customer',
+              'play_store_url': 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.snagninja.app',
+              'app_link': 'snagninja://invites/received',
+            },
+          );
+        } catch (e) {
+          debugPrint('Invite email function failed: $e');
+        }
+
+        if (mounted) {
+          _emailController.clear();
+          // Refresh the sent invitations provider so the 'Sent' tab is up to date
+          ref.read(sentConnectionInvitationsProvider.notifier).fetchInvitations();
+          // Pop back — parent screen will show its own success snackbar
+          Navigator.pop(context, true);
+        }
+      }
+    } catch (e) {
+      if (mounted) {
+        CustomSnackBar.showError(
+          context: context,
+          message: 'Error sending invite: $e',
+        );
+      }
+    } finally {
+      if (mounted) {
+        setState(() {
+          _isInviting = false;
+        });
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final currentTheme = ref.watch(themeProvider);
+    final theme = AppTheme(currentTheme);
+    AppSizes.init(context);
+
+    return Scaffold(
+      appBar: AppBar(
+        leading: IconButton(
+          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
+          onPressed: () {
+            Navigator.pop(context);
+          },
+        ),
+        title: Padding(
+          padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: AppSizes.extraSmallSpace),
+          child: Text(
+            'Invite Customer',
+            style: TextStyle(
+              fontSize: AppSizes.appBarTitleSize,
+              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              color: Colors.white,
+            ),
+          ),
+        ),
+        backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
+        elevation: 0,
+        toolbarHeight: AppSizes.appBarHeight,
+      ),
+      backgroundColor: theme.scaffoldBackground,
+
+      body: SingleChildScrollView(
+        padding: AppSizes.screenPadding,
+        child: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Text(
+              'Customer Name',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                color: theme.textColor,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            CustomTextField(
+              controller: _nameController,
+              hintText: 'Enter customer name',
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            Text(
+              'Email',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                color: theme.textColor,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            CustomTextField(
+              controller: _emailController,
+              hintText: 'Enter email',
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            Text(
+              'Phone Number (Optional)',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                color: theme.textColor,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            CustomTextField(
+              controller: _phoneController,
+              hintText: 'Enter phone number',
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.largeSpace),
+
+            Center(
+              child: SizedBox(
+                width: AppSizes.buttonSize.width,
+                height: AppSizes.buttonSize.height,
+                child: ElevatedButton(
+                  onPressed: _isInviting ? null : _handleInvite,
+                  style: AppStyles.primaryButtonStyleLogin.copyWith(
+                    backgroundColor: WidgetStateProperty.all(
+                      theme.primaryColor,
+                    ),
+                    minimumSize: WidgetStateProperty.all(AppSizes.buttonSize),
+                  ),
+                  child:
+                      _isInviting
+                          ? const SizedBox(
+                            height: 20,
+                            width: 20,
+                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
+                              color: Colors.white,
+                              strokeWidth: 2,
+                            ),
+                          )
+                          : Text(
+                            'SEND INVITE',
+                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: AppSizes.buttonText),
+                          ),
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            Center(
+              child: SizedBox(
+                width: AppSizes.buttonSize.width,
+                height: AppSizes.buttonSize.height,
+                child: OutlinedButton(
+                  style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
+                    side: BorderSide(color: theme.primaryColor),
+                    shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
+                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(
+                        AppSizes.containerRadius,
+                      ),
+                    ),
+                  ),
+                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
+                  child: Text(
+                    'CANCEL',
+                    style: TextStyle(
+                      fontSize: AppSizes.buttonText,
+                      color: theme.primaryColor,
+                    ),
+                  ),
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+          ],
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}
```
````

````
@@ -0,0 +1,303 @@
+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/colors.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/sizes.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/widgets/account_type_selector.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/widgets/custom_textfield.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/providers/app_providers.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/providers/api_providers.dart';
+import '../widgets/custom_snackbar.dart';
+import 'home_screen.dart';
+
+/// Step 3 of Sign Up: Profile Completion
+class ProfileSetupScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
+  const ProfileSetupScreen({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  ConsumerState<ProfileSetupScreen> createState() => _ProfileSetupScreenState();
+}
+
+class _ProfileSetupScreenState extends ConsumerState<ProfileSetupScreen> {
+  final _firstNameController = TextEditingController();
+  final _lastNameController = TextEditingController();
+  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
+  final _companyController = TextEditingController();
+
+  bool _isLoading = false;
+
+  bool _validateFields() {
+    if (_firstNameController.text.trim().isEmpty ||
+        _lastNameController.text.trim().isEmpty ||
+        _phoneController.text.trim().isEmpty) {
+      CustomSnackBar.showError(
+        context: context,
+        message: 'Please fill in all mandatory fields',
+      );
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    final authState = ref.read(authProvider);
+    if (authState.accountType == 'Builder' &&
+        _companyController.text.trim().isEmpty) {
+      CustomSnackBar.showError(
+        context: context,
+        message: 'Please enter your company name',
+      );
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    if (_phoneController.text.trim().length < 8) {
+      CustomSnackBar.showError(
+        context: context,
+        message: 'Please enter a valid phone number',
+      );
+      return false;
+    }
+
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _firstNameController.dispose();
+    _lastNameController.dispose();
+    _phoneController.dispose();
+    _companyController.dispose();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    AppSizes.init(context);
+    final authState = ref.watch(authProvider);
+
+    return Scaffold(
+      appBar: AppBar(
+        title: Text(
+          'Complete Profile (3/3)',
+          style: TextStyle(
+            fontSize: AppSizes.appBarTitleSize,
+            color: Colors.white,
+            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+          ),
+        ),
+        backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
+        toolbarHeight: AppSizes.appBarHeight,
+        automaticallyImplyLeading: false, // User must complete profile
+      ),
+      body: SingleChildScrollView(
+        padding: AppSizes.screenPadding,
+        child: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Text(
+              'Finalize Your Profile',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                color: AppColors.primaryColor,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace),
+            Text(
+              'Step 3: Tell us a bit more about yourself to personalize your experience.',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.bodyText,
+                color: Colors.grey[600],
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.largeSpace),
+
+            // Account Type Selector
+            Text(
+              'I am a...',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace),
+            AccountTypeSelector(
+              onSelected: (type) {
+                // Handled by widget and provider
+              },
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            Text(
+              authState.accountDescription ?? 'Select an account type',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.subtitleText,
+                color: Colors.grey,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            // First Name
+            Text(
+              'First Name',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            CustomTextField(
+              controller: _firstNameController,
+              hintText: 'Enter your first name',
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.words,
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            // Last Name
+            Text(
+              'Last Name',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            CustomTextField(
+              controller: _lastNameController,
+              hintText: 'Enter your last name',
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.words,
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            // Phone Number (Mandatory)
+            Text(
+              'Phone Number',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            CustomTextField(
+              controller: _phoneController,
+              hintText: 'Enter your phone number',
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            // Company Name (Optional for Builders)
+            if (authState.accountType == 'Builder') ...[
+              Text(
+                'Company Name',
+                style: TextStyle(
+                  fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                ),
+              ),
+              SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+              CustomTextField(
+                controller: _companyController,
+                hintText: 'Enter your company name',
+                height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+                textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.words,
+              ),
+              SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+            ],
+
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.largeSpace),
+
+            // Complete Button
+            SizedBox(
+              width: double.infinity,
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              child: ElevatedButton(
+                onPressed:
+                    _isLoading
+                        ? null
+                        : () async {
+                          if (!_validateFields()) return;
+
+                          setState(() => _isLoading = true);
+                          final navigator = Navigator.of(context);
+                          final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
+
+                          try {
+                            final apiService = ref.read(apiProvider);
+                            final success = await apiService.completeProfile(
+                              firstName: _firstNameController.text.trim(),
+                              lastName: _lastNameController.text.trim(),
+                              phone: _phoneController.text.trim(),
+                              accountType: authState.accountType ?? 'Customer',
+                              company:
+                                  authState.accountType == 'Builder'
+                                      ? _companyController.text.trim()
+                                      : null,
+                            );
+
+                            if (success) {
+                              // Refresh the auth state to include the new profile data
+                              await ref.read(authProvider.notifier).refresh();
+
+                              if (!mounted) return;
+                              CustomSnackBar.showSuccessViaMessenger(
+                                messenger: messenger,
+                                message: 'Profile completed successfully!',
+                              );
+
+                              navigator.pushReplacement(
+                                MaterialPageRoute(
+                                  builder: (context) => const HomeScreen(),
+                                ),
+                              );
+                            } else {
+                              if (!mounted) return;
+                              CustomSnackBar.showErrorViaMessenger(
+                                messenger: messenger,
+                                message:
+                                    'Failed to save profile. Please try again.',
+                              );
+                            }
+                          } catch (e) {
+                            if (!mounted) return;
+                            CustomSnackBar.showErrorViaMessenger(
+                              messenger: messenger,
+                              message: e.toString().replaceFirst(
+                                'Exception: ',
+                                '',
+                              ),
+                            );
+                          } finally {
+                            if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
+                          }
+                        },
+                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
+                  backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
+                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
+                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
+                  ),
+                ),
+                child:
+                    _isLoading
+                        ? const SizedBox(
+                          width: 24,
+                          height: 24,
+                          child: CircularProgressIndicator(
+                            color: Colors.white,
+                            strokeWidth: 2.5,
+                          ),
+                        )
+                        : Text(
+                          'Complete Setup',
+                          style: TextStyle(
+                            fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                            color: Colors.white,
+                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                          ),
+                        ),
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: 40),
+          ],
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}
````

````
@@ -0,0 +1,228 @@
+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/colors.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/sizes.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/widgets/custom_textfield.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/providers/api_providers.dart';
+import '../widgets/custom_snackbar.dart';
+import 'profile_setup_screen.dart';
+
+/// Step 2 of Sign Up: Email Verification
+class VerificationScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
+  final String email;
+
+  const VerificationScreen({super.key, required this.email});
+
+  @override
+  ConsumerState<VerificationScreen> createState() => _VerificationScreenState();
+}
+
+class _VerificationScreenState extends ConsumerState<VerificationScreen> {
+  final _codeController = TextEditingController();
+  bool _isLoading = false;
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _codeController.dispose();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    AppSizes.init(context);
+
+    return Scaffold(
+      appBar: AppBar(
+        leading: IconButton(
+          icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white),
+          onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
+        ),
+        title: Padding(
+          padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: AppSizes.extraSmallSpace),
+          child: Text(
+            'Verify Email (2/3)',
+            style: TextStyle(
+              fontSize: AppSizes.appBarTitleSize,
+              color: Colors.white,
+              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+            ),
+          ),
+        ),
+        backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
+        toolbarHeight: AppSizes.appBarHeight,
+      ),
+      body: SingleChildScrollView(
+        padding: AppSizes.screenPadding,
+        child: Column(
+          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+          children: [
+            Text(
+              'Confirm Your Identity',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                color: AppColors.primaryColor,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace),
+            Text(
+              'Step 2: We have sent a 6-digit verification code to ${widget.email}. Please enter it below.',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.bodyText,
+                color: Colors.grey[600],
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.largeSpace),
+
+            // OTP Code Field
+            Text(
+              'Verification Code',
+              style: TextStyle(
+                fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace / 2),
+            CustomTextField(
+              controller: _codeController,
+              hintText: 'Enter 6-digit code',
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
+              maxLength: 6,
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+
+            // Resend Button
+            Center(
+              child: TextButton(
+                onPressed:
+                    _isLoading
+                        ? null
+                        : () async {
+                          setState(() => _isLoading = true);
+                          final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
+                          try {
+                            final apiService = ref.read(apiProvider);
+                            final success = await apiService
+                                .sendVerificationCode(widget.email);
+                            if (success) {
+                              if (!mounted) return;
+                              CustomSnackBar.showSuccessViaMessenger(
+                                messenger: messenger,
+                                message:
+                                    'A new code has been sent to your email.',
+                              );
+                            }
+                          } catch (e) {
+                            if (!mounted) return;
+                            CustomSnackBar.showErrorViaMessenger(
+                              messenger: messenger,
+                              message: e.toString(),
+                            );
+                          } finally {
+                            if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
+                          }
+                        },
+                child: Text(
+                  'Resend Code',
+                  style: TextStyle(
+                    color: AppColors.primaryColor,
+                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                    fontSize: AppSizes.bodyText,
+                  ),
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+            SizedBox(height: AppSizes.largeSpace),
+
+            // Verify Button
+            SizedBox(
+              width: double.infinity,
+              height: AppSizes.inputFieldHeight,
+              child: ElevatedButton(
+                onPressed:
+                    _isLoading
+                        ? null
+                        : () async {
+                          if (_codeController.text.length != 6) {
+                            CustomSnackBar.showError(
+                              context: context,
+                              message: 'Please enter a 6-digit code',
+                            );
+                            return;
+                          }
+
+                          setState(() => _isLoading = true);
+                          final navigator = Navigator.of(context);
+                          final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
+
+                          try {
+                            final apiService = ref.read(apiProvider);
+                            final success = await apiService.verifyCode(
+                              widget.email,
+                              _codeController.text.trim(),
+                            );
+
+                            if (success) {
+                              if (!mounted) return;
+                              navigator.pushAndRemoveUntil(
+                                MaterialPageRoute(
+                                  builder:
+                                      (context) => const ProfileSetupScreen(),
+                                ),
+                                (route) =>
+                                    false, // Remove all previous routes to prevent going back to signup
+                              );
+                            } else {
+                              if (!mounted) return;
+                              CustomSnackBar.showErrorViaMessenger(
+                                messenger: messenger,
+                                message:
+                                    'Invalid or expired verification code.',
+                              );
+                            }
+                          } catch (e) {
+                            if (!mounted) return;
+                            CustomSnackBar.showErrorViaMessenger(
+                              messenger: messenger,
+                              message: e.toString().replaceFirst(
+                                'Exception: ',
+                                '',
+                              ),
+                            );
+                          } finally {
+                            if (mounted) setState(() => _isLoading = false);
+                          }
+                        },
+                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
+                  backgroundColor: AppColors.primaryColor,
+                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
+                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
+                  ),
+                ),
+                child:
+                    _isLoading
+                        ? const SizedBox(
+                          width: 24,
+                          height: 24,
+                          child: CircularProgressIndicator(
+                            color: Colors.white,
+                            strokeWidth: 2.5,
+                          ),
+                        )
+                        : Text(
+                          'Verify & Continue',
+                          style: TextStyle(
+                            fontSize: AppSizes.titleText,
+                            color: Colors.white,
+                            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                          ),
+                        ),
+              ),
+            ),
+          ],
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}
````

````
# File: lib/services/brand_service.dart

## Changes (+128/-0)

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,128 @@
+import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
+import '../models/brand_config.dart';
+
+/// Centralized service managing multi-region dynamic branding (US, UK, Global ROW).
+class BrandService {
+  static final SupabaseClient _supabase = Supabase.instance.client;
+
+  /// Detects the initial regional brand based on the device's native OS locale.
+  /// Used pre-login before user authenticates.
+  static BrandConfig detectBrandFromDeviceLocale() {
+    try {
+      final locale = PlatformDispatcher.instance.locale;
+      final countryCode = locale.countryCode?.toUpperCase().trim() ?? '';
+      
+      debugPrint('🌐 Native Device Locale: $locale (Country: $countryCode)');
+
+      if (countryCode == 'US') {
+        return BrandConfig.punchListUs;
+      } else if (countryCode == 'GB' || countryCode == 'UK') {
+        return BrandConfig.snagUk;
+      } else {
+        // Rest of World (ROW) default
+        return BrandConfig.defectGlobal;
+      }
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error detecting device locale: $e');
+      return BrandConfig.snagUk;
+    }
+  }
+
+  /// Resolves the authenticated user's permanent brand_id from Supabase auth metadata or DB profile.
+  /// If unauthenticated or no brand_id tag is found, falls back to device locale.
+  static Future<BrandConfig> resolveUserBrand() async {
+    final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+    if (user == null) {
+      return detectBrandFromDeviceLocale();
+    }
+
+    // 1. Check Auth Metadata
+    final metaBrand = user.userMetadata?['brand_id']?.toString();
+    if (metaBrand != null && metaBrand.isNotEmpty) {
+      debugPrint('👤 Brand resolved from user auth metadata: $metaBrand');
+      return BrandConfig.fromString(metaBrand);
+    }
+
+    // 2. Check user_profiles table in Supabase PostgreSQL
+    try {
+      final profile = await _supabase
+          .from('user_profiles')
+          .select('brand_id')
+          .eq('id', user.id)
+          .maybeSingle();
+
+      if (profile != null && profile['brand_id'] != null) {
+        final dbBrand = profile['brand_id'].toString();
+        debugPrint('👤 Brand resolved from user_profiles DB: $dbBrand');
+        
+        // Backfill metadata for fast subsequent loads
+        await _supabase.auth.updateUser(
+          UserAttributes(data: {'brand_id': dbBrand}),
+        );
+        return BrandConfig.fromString(dbBrand);
+      }
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error reading brand_id from user_profiles: $e');
+    }
+
+    // 3. Fallback: Determine brand from device locale and lock it to user's profile
+    final initialBrand = detectBrandFromDeviceLocale();
+    try {
+      await _supabase.auth.updateUser(
+        UserAttributes(data: {'brand_id': initialBrand.brandId.id}),
+      );
+      await _supabase.from('user_profiles').upsert({
+        'id': user.id,
+        'brand_id': initialBrand.brandId.id,
+      });
+      debugPrint('📌 Locked user ${user.id} to brand: ${initialBrand.brandId.id}');
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Warning: Could not lock brand_id to user profile: $e');
+    }
+
+    return initialBrand;
+  }
+}
+
+/// Riverpod StateNotifier managing active brand configuration
+class BrandNotifier extends StateNotifier<BrandConfig> {
+  BrandNotifier() : super(BrandService.detectBrandFromDeviceLocale()) {
+    init();
+  }
+
+  Future<void> init() async {
+    final config = await BrandService.resolveUserBrand();
+    state = config;
+  }
+
+  void setBrand(BrandConfig config) {
+    state = config;
+  }
+
+  Future<void> syncUserBrand(String brandIdString) async {
+    final config = BrandConfig.fromString(brandIdString);
+    state = config;
+    
+    final user = Supabase.instance.client.auth.currentUser;
+    if (user != null) {
+      try {
+        await Supabase.instance.client.auth.updateUser(
+          UserAttributes(data: {'brand_id': config.brandId.id}),
+        );
+        await Supabase.instance.client.from('user_profiles').upsert({
+          'id': user.id,
+          'brand_id': config.brandId.id,
+        });
+      } catch (e) {
+        debugPrint('Error syncing user brand: $e');
+      }
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+/// Global Riverpod Provider for accessing active BrandConfig anywhere in the app
+final brandProvider = StateNotifierProvider<BrandNotifier, BrandConfig>((ref) {
+  return BrandNotifier();
+});
```
````

````
@@ -0,0 +1,106 @@
+import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_local_notifications/flutter_local_notifications.dart';
+
+typedef NotificationTapCallback = void Function(String? payload);
+
+/// Centralized service for triggering native System Notifications
+/// in the mobile Notification Center (Status Bar / Lock Screen / Notification Shade)
+/// like WhatsApp, Discord, etc.
+class LocalNotificationService {
+  static final FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin _notificationsPlugin =
+      FlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin();
+
+  static bool _isInitialized = false;
+  static NotificationTapCallback? onNotificationTap;
+
+  /// Initialize local notifications and request permissions.
+  static Future<void> initialize() async {
+    if (_isInitialized) return;
+
+    const AndroidInitializationSettings initializationSettingsAndroid =
+        AndroidInitializationSettings('@mipmap/ic_launcher');
+
+    const DarwinInitializationSettings initializationSettingsIOS =
+        DarwinInitializationSettings(
+      requestAlertPermission: true,
+      requestBadgePermission: true,
+      requestSoundPermission: true,
+    );
+
+    const InitializationSettings initializationSettings = InitializationSettings(
+      android: initializationSettingsAndroid,
+      iOS: initializationSettingsIOS,
+    );
+
+    await _notificationsPlugin.initialize(
+      settings: initializationSettings,
+      onDidReceiveNotificationResponse: (NotificationResponse details) {
+        debugPrint('System notification tapped with payload: ${details.payload}');
+        if (onNotificationTap != null) {
+          onNotificationTap!(details.payload);
+        }
+      },
+    );
+
+    // Request Notification permission on Android 13+ (API 33+)
+    try {
+      final androidImplementation = _notificationsPlugin
+          .resolvePlatformSpecificImplementation<
+              AndroidFlutterLocalNotificationsPlugin>();
+      if (androidImplementation != null) {
+        await androidImplementation.requestNotificationsPermission();
+      }
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Warning: Could not request Android notification permission: $e');
+    }
+
+    _isInitialized = true;
+    debugPrint('✅ LocalNotificationService initialized for System Notification Center');
+  }
+
+  /// Show a native system notification in the mobile Notification Center.
+  static Future<void> showNotification({
+    required int id,
+    required String title,
+    required String message,
+    String? payload,
+  }) async {
+    if (!_isInitialized) {
+      await initialize();
+    }
+
+    const AndroidNotificationDetails androidDetails = AndroidNotificationDetails(
+      'snagninja_system_channel',
+      'SnagNinja Notifications',
+      channelDescription: 'Real-time notifications for SnagNinja reports and invites',
+      importance: Importance.max,
+      priority: Priority.high,
+      showWhen: true,
+      enableVibration: true,
+      playSound: true,
+      icon: '@mipmap/ic_launcher',
+    );
+
+    const NotificationDetails platformDetails = NotificationDetails(
+      android: androidDetails,
+      iOS: DarwinNotificationDetails(
+        presentAlert: true,
+        presentBadge: true,
+        presentSound: true,
+      ),
+    );
+
+    try {
+      await _notificationsPlugin.show(
+        id: id,
+        title: title,
+        body: message,
+        notificationDetails: platformDetails,
+        payload: payload,
+      );
+      debugPrint('🔔 System Notification displayed in Notification Center: $title');
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error showing system notification: $e');
+    }
+  }
+}
````

````
@@ -0,0 +1,297 @@
+import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
+import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';
+
+// TODO: Replace with real Play Store URL once SnagNinja is published
+const String _kPlayStoreUrl =
+    'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.snagninja.app';
+
+/// Centralized service to send email + in-app notifications to recipients.
+/// Note: Native local push notifications are delivered to the recipient in real-time
+/// via Supabase Realtime subscription on the `notifications` table in [NotificationsNotifier].
+class NotificationService {
+  static final _supabase = Supabase.instance.client;
+
+  // ─── Email via Edge Function ──────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+  /// Sends an email notification via the `send-notification-email` Edge Function.
+  static Future<void> sendEmailNotification({
+    required String to,
+    required String eventType,
+    required String senderName,
+    String? recipientName,
+    String? reportTitle,
+    String? propertyAddress,
+    String? reportId,
+    String? appLink, // deep link e.g. snagninja://invites
+    String? playStoreUrl,
+  }) async {
+    try {
+      await _supabase.functions.invoke(
+        'send-notification-email',
+        body: {
+          'to': to,
+          'event_type': eventType,
+          'sender_name': senderName,
+          'recipient_name': recipientName ?? '',
+          'report_title': reportTitle ?? 'Snagging Report',
+          'property_address': propertyAddress ?? '',
+          'report_id': reportId ?? '',
+          'app_link': appLink ?? '',
+          'play_store_url': playStoreUrl ?? _kPlayStoreUrl,
+        },
+      );
+      debugPrint('📧 Notification email sent: $eventType → $to');
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('⚠️ Email notification failed ($eventType): $e');
+    }
+  }
+
+  // ─── In-App via Supabase `notifications` table ────────────────────────────
+
+  /// Inserts an in-app notification into the `notifications` table for the recipient.
+  static Future<void> insertInAppNotification({
+    required String recipientEmail,
+    required String title,
+    required String message,
+    String? reportId,
+  }) async {
+    final email = recipientEmail.toLowerCase().trim();
+    final now = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
+    debugPrint('🔔 Inserting in-app notification for recipient: $email');
+
+    String? recipientUserId;
+    try {
+      final profile = await _supabase
+          .from('user_profiles')
+          .select('id')
+          .ilike('email', email)
+          .maybeSingle();
+      if (profile != null && profile['id'] != null) {
+        recipientUserId = profile['id'].toString();
+      }
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Could not resolve user_id for $email: $e');
+    }
+
+    try {
+      await _supabase.from('notifications').insert({
+        'user_id': recipientUserId,
+        'title': title,
+        'message': message,
+        'report_id': reportId,
+        'is_read': false,
+        'created_at': now,
+      });
+      debugPrint('✅ In-app notification inserted for recipient user_id ($recipientUserId)');
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Note: In-app notification insertion: $e');
+    }
+  }
+
+  // ─── Event Methods (Dispatches to Recipient) ──────────────────────────────
+
+  /// Notifies a user (builder or customer) that they received a connection invite.
+  static Future<void> notifyInviteReceived({
+    required String recipientEmail,
+    required String senderName,   // e.g. "John (Builder)"
+    required String senderRole,   // 'Builder' or 'Customer'
+  }) async {
+    const title = '🤝 New Connection Invitation';
+    final message =
+        '$senderName has sent you a connection invitation on SnagNinja. '
+        'Open the app to accept or decline.';
+
+    await Future.wait([
+      insertInAppNotification(
+        recipientEmail: recipientEmail,
+        title: title,
+        message: message,
+      ),
+      sendEmailNotification(
+        to: recipientEmail,
+        eventType: 'invite_received',
+        senderName: senderName,
+        appLink: 'snagninja://invites/received',
+        playStoreUrl: _kPlayStoreUrl,
+      ),
+    ]);
+  }
+
+  /// Notifies the original inviter when their invitation is accepted or declined.
+  static Future<void> notifyInviteResponse({
+    required String inviterEmail,
+    required String responderName,  // e.g. "Jane (Customer)"
+    required String responderRole,
+    required bool accepted,
+  }) async {
+    final statusText = accepted ? 'accepted' : 'declined';
+    final actionEmoji = accepted ? '✅' : '❌';
+    final title = '$actionEmoji Invitation ${accepted ? 'Accepted' : 'Declined'}';
+    final message =
+        '$responderName has $statusText your connection invitation on SnagNinja.';
+
+    await Future.wait([
+      sendEmailNotification(
+        to: inviterEmail,
+        eventType: accepted ? 'invite_accepted' : 'invite_declined',
+        senderName: responderName,
+        reportTitle: message,
+        appLink: 'snagninja://invites/sent',
+        playStoreUrl: _kPlayStoreUrl,
+      ),
+      insertInAppNotification(
+        recipientEmail: inviterEmail,
+        title: title,
+        message: message,
+      ),
+    ]);
+  }
+
+  /// Notifies builder that customer has shared a report with them.
+  static Future<void> notifyBuilderReportShared({
+    required String builderEmail,
+    required String customerName,   // e.g. "Jane Smith (Customer)"
+    String? builderName,
+    String? reportTitle,
+    String? propertyAddress,
+    String? reportId,
+  }) async {
+    const title = '📋 New Snagging Report Shared';
+    final message =
+        '$customerName has shared a snagging report with you${reportTitle != null ? ': "$reportTitle"' : ''}. '
+        'Open the app to review it.';
+
+    await Future.wait([
+      sendEmailNotification(
+        to: builderEmail,
+        eventType: 'report_shared',
+        senderName: customerName,
+        recipientName: builderName,
+        reportTitle: reportTitle,
+        propertyAddress: propertyAddress,
+        reportId: reportId,
+        appLink: reportId != null
+            ? 'snagninja://reports/$reportId'
+            : 'snagninja://reports',
+        playStoreUrl: _kPlayStoreUrl,
+      ),
+      insertInAppNotification(
+        recipientEmail: builderEmail,
+        title: title,
+        message: message,
+        reportId: reportId,
+      ),
+    ]);
+  }
+
+  /// Notifies customer that builder submitted report for review.
+  static Future<void> notifyCustomerReportSubmittedForReview({
+    required String customerEmail,
+    required String builderName,
+    String? customerName,
+    String? reportTitle,
+    String? propertyAddress,
+    String? reportId,
+  }) async {
+    const title = '📋 Report Ready for Review';
+    final message =
+        '$builderName has submitted "${reportTitle ?? 'Snagging Report'}" '
+        'for your review. Please open the app to sign off.';
+
+    await Future.wait([
+      sendEmailNotification(
+        to: customerEmail,
+        eventType: 'report_submitted_for_review',
+        senderName: builderName,
+        recipientName: customerName,
+        reportTitle: reportTitle,
+        propertyAddress: propertyAddress,
+        reportId: reportId,
+        appLink: reportId != null
+            ? 'snagninja://reports/$reportId'
+            : 'snagninja://reports',
+        playStoreUrl: _kPlayStoreUrl,
+      ),
+      insertInAppNotification(
+        recipientEmail: customerEmail,
+        title: title,
+        message: message,
+        reportId: reportId,
+      ),
+    ]);
+  }
+
+  /// Notifies builder that customer has added a new issue.
+  static Future<void> notifyBuilderNewIssueAdded({
+    required String builderEmail,
+    required String customerName,
+    String? builderName,
+    String? reportTitle,
+    String? propertyAddress,
+    String? reportId,
+  }) async {
+    const title = '🔔 New Issue Added to Report';
+    final message =
+        '$customerName has added a new issue to "${reportTitle ?? 'Snagging Report'}". '
+        'The report is now back in Active status. Please review and address the new issue.';
+
+    await Future.wait([
+      sendEmailNotification(
+        to: builderEmail,
+        eventType: 'new_issue_added',
+        senderName: customerName,
+        recipientName: builderName,
+        reportTitle: reportTitle,
+        propertyAddress: propertyAddress,
+        reportId: reportId,
+        appLink: reportId != null
+            ? 'snagninja://reports/$reportId'
+            : 'snagninja://reports',
+        playStoreUrl: _kPlayStoreUrl,
+      ),
+      insertInAppNotification(
+        recipientEmail: builderEmail,
+        title: title,
+        message: message,
+        reportId: reportId,
+      ),
+    ]);
+  }
+
+  /// Notifies builder that customer has signed off and completed the report.
+  static Future<void> notifyBuilderReportCompleted({
+    required String builderEmail,
+    required String customerName,
+    String? builderName,
+    String? reportTitle,
+    String? propertyAddress,
+    String? reportId,
+  }) async {
+    const title = '🎉 Report Signed Off & Completed!';
+    final message =
+        '$customerName has signed off "${reportTitle ?? 'Snagging Report'}". '
+        'Excellent work! The snagging report is now closed. Thank you! 🙌';
+
+    await Future.wait([
+      sendEmailNotification(
+        to: builderEmail,
+        eventType: 'report_completed',
+        senderName: customerName,
+        recipientName: builderName,
+        reportTitle: reportTitle,
+        propertyAddress: propertyAddress,
+        reportId: reportId,
+        appLink: reportId != null
+            ? 'snagninja://reports/completed/$reportId'
+            : 'snagninja://reports/completed',
+        playStoreUrl: _kPlayStoreUrl,
+      ),
+      insertInAppNotification(
+        recipientEmail: builderEmail,
+        title: title,
+        message: message,
+        reportId: reportId,
+      ),
+    ]);
+  }
+}
````

````
+              ),
+            ),
+            pw.SizedBox(height: 8),
+          ],
+          // Builder Fix Image (if present)
+          if (item.builderImagePath != null && item.builderImagePath!.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[
+            pw.Text(
+              'BUILDER FIX PHOTO:',
+              style: pw.TextStyle(fontSize: 10, fontWeight: pw.FontWeight.bold, color: PdfColors.grey700),
+            ),
+            pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
+            _buildImageWithOverlay(item.builderImagePath!, 'FIX PHOTO'),
+            pw.SizedBox(height: 8),
+          ],
           pw.Divider(color: PdfColors.grey300),
         ],
       ),
```
````

````
+/// Checks if an accepted or declined invitation is older than 24 hours.
+/// Pending invitations never expire.
+bool isInviteExpired24h(Map<String, dynamic> inv) {
+  final status = inv['status']?.toString().toLowerCase();
+  if (status == 'pending' || status == null) return false;
+
+  final dateStr = inv['updated_at'] ?? inv['created_at'];
+  if (dateStr == null) return false;
+  final updatedAt = DateTime.tryParse(dateStr.toString());
+  if (updatedAt == null) return false;
+
+  return DateTime.now().difference(updatedAt.toLocal()).inHours >= 24;
+}

+
+      // Resolve regional brand from device locale if not already set
+      final metaBrand = user.userMetadata?['brand_id']?.toString();
+      final brandId = (metaBrand != null && metaBrand.isNotEmpty)

+          'company': company ?? '',

+        }
+      }
+
+      return true;
````
