+  /* ========== Builder Connection Invitations ========== */
+
+  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getSentConnectionInvitations() async {
+    try {
+      final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+      if (user == null) return [];
+      
+      final accountType = user.userMetadata?['account_type'];
+      
+      List<Map<String, dynamic>> results;
+      if (accountType == 'Builder') {
+        final response = await _supabase
+            .from('customer_invitations')
+            .select('*')
+            .eq('builder_id', user.id);
+        results = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+        
+        // Manual resolution for customer (recipient)
+        for (var i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
+          try {
+            final email = results[i]['customer_email'];
+            final profile = await _supabase
+                .from('user_profiles')
+                .select('first_name, last_name, email, avatar_url')
+                .eq('email', email)
+                .maybeSingle();
+            if (profile != null) {
+              results[i]['customer'] = profile;
+            }
+          } catch (_) {}
+        }
+      } else {
+        final response = await _supabase
+            .from('builder_invitations')
+            .select('*')
+            .eq('customer_id', user.id);
+        results = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+        
+        // Manual resolution for builder (recipient)
+        for (var i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
+          try {
+            final email = results[i]['builder_email'];
+            final profile = await _supabase
+                .from('user_profiles')
+                .select('first_name, last_name, email, avatar_url')
+                .eq('email', email)
+                .maybeSingle();
+            if (profile != null) {
+              results[i]['builder'] = profile;
+            }
+          } catch (_) {}
+        }
+      }
+      // Filter out accepted or declined invitations older than 24 hours
+      return results.where((inv) => !isInviteExpired24h(inv)).toList();
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error getting sent connection invitations: $e');
+      return [];
+    }
+  }
+
+  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getSentReportInvitations() async {
+    try {
+      final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+      if (user == null) return [];
+      
+      final response = await _supabase
+          .from('builder_report_invitations')
+          .select('*, reports(*, property:property_id(address))')
+          .eq('customer_id', user.id);
+          
+      final results = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+      
+      // Manual resolution for builders
+      for (var i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
+        if (results[i]['builder'] == null) {
+          try {
+            final email = results[i]['builder_email'];
+            final profile = await _supabase
+                .from('user_profiles')
+                .select('first_name, last_name, email, avatar_url')
+                .eq('email', email)
+                .maybeSingle();
+            if (profile != null) {
+              results[i]['builder'] = profile;
+            }
+          } catch (_) {}
+        }
+      }
+      
+      // Filter out accepted or declined invitations older than 24 hours
+      return results.where((inv) => !isInviteExpired24h(inv)).toList();
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error getting sent report invitations: $e');
+      return [];
+    }
+  }
+
+  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getReceivedCustomerInvitations() async {
+    try {
+      final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+      final email = user?.email;
+      if (email == null) return [];
+      
+      debugPrint('Fetching received customer invitations for: $email');
+      
+      // Builder invites Customer -> Customer sees it
+      final response = await _supabase
+          .from('customer_invitations')
+          .select('*, builder:user_profiles!builder_id(first_name, last_name, email)')
+          .ilike('customer_email', email)
+          .eq('status', 'Pending');
+      
+      debugPrint('Found ${response.length} received customer invitations');
+      return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error getting received customer invitations: $e');
+      return [];
+    }
+  }
+
+  Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> getReceivedConnectionInvitations() async {
+    try {
+      final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+      final email = user?.email;
+      if (email == null) {
+        debugPrint('Fetch Received Invites: No logged in user email');
+        return [];
+      }
+      
+      final accountType = user?.userMetadata?['account_type'];
+      debugPrint('DEBUG: Current User ID: ${user?.id}');
+      debugPrint('DEBUG: Current User Email: $email');
+      debugPrint('DEBUG: Current User Metadata: ${user?.userMetadata}');
+      debugPrint('Fetching received invitations for: $email ($accountType)');
+      
+      List<Map<String, dynamic>> results;
+      if (accountType == 'Builder') {
+        // Builder receives from builder_invitations (Customer -> Builder)
+        final response = await _supabase
+            .from('builder_invitations')
+            .select('*, customer:user_profiles(first_name, last_name, email)')
+            .ilike('builder_email', email)
+            .eq('status', 'Pending');
+        results = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+        
+        // Manual resolution for missing profiles
+        for (var i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
+          if (results[i]['customer'] == null) {
+            final custId = results[i]['customer_id'];
+            debugPrint('DEBUG: Manual resolution for customer_id: $custId');
+            try {
+              final profile = await _supabase
+                  .from('user_profiles')
+                  .select('first_name, last_name, email')
+                  .eq('id', custId)
+                  .maybeSingle();
+              if (profile != null) {
+                results[i]['customer'] = profile;
+                debugPrint('DEBUG: Resolved customer profile: $profile');
+              }
+            } catch (e) {
+              debugPrint('DEBUG: Failed to manually resolve customer: $e');
+            }
+          }
+        }
+      } else {
+        // Customer receives from customer_invitations (Builder -> Customer)
+        final response = await _supabase
+            .from('customer_invitations')
+            .select('*, builder:user_profiles(first_name, last_name, email)')
+            .ilike('customer_email', email)
+            .eq('status', 'Pending');
+        results = List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+        
+        // Manual resolution for missing profiles
+        for (var i = 0; i < results.length; i++) {
+          if (results[i]['builder'] == null) {
+            final builderId = results[i]['builder_id'];
+            try {
+              final profile = await _supabase
+                  .from('user_profiles')
+                  .select('first_name, last_name, email')
+                  .eq('id', builderId)
+                  .maybeSingle();
+              if (profile != null) {
+                results[i]['builder'] = profile;
+              }
+            } catch (e) {
+              debugPrint('DEBUG: Failed to manually resolve builder: $e');
+            }
+          }
+        }
+      }
+      return results;
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error getting received connection invitations: $e');
+      // If the join fails, it might be due to missing foreign key relationships in Supabase
+      // Try a simpler query as fallback
+      try {
+        final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+        final email = user?.email;
+        final accountType = user?.userMetadata?['account_type'];
+        if (accountType == 'Builder') {
+          final response = await _supabase
+              .from('builder_invitations')
+              .select('*')
+              .ilike('builder_email', email!)
+              .eq('status', 'Pending');
+          debugPrint('Fallback fetch (no join) successful: $response');
+          return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+        } else {
+          final response = await _supabase
+              .from('customer_invitations')
+              .select('*')
+              .ilike('customer_email', email!)
+              .eq('status', 'Pending');
+          debugPrint('Fallback fetch (no join) successful: $response');
+          return List<Map<String, dynamic>>.from(response);
+        }
+      } catch (e2) {
+        debugPrint('Fallback fetch also failed: $e2');
+        return [];
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  Future<bool> sendConnectionInvitation({
+    required String builderEmail,
+    required String builderName,
+  }) async {
+    try {
+      final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+      if (user == null) throw Exception('Not logged in');
+
+      // Check if already invited or connected
+      final existingInvite = await _supabase
+          .from('builder_invitations')
+          .select('id')
+          .eq('customer_id', user.id)
+          .eq('builder_email', builderEmail)
+          .eq('status', 'Pending')
+          .maybeSingle();
+
+      if (existingInvite != null) {
+        throw Exception('An invitation has already been sent to this email.');
+      }
+
+      await _supabase.from('builder_invitations').insert({
+        'customer_id': user.id,
+        'builder_email': builderEmail,
+        'builder_name': builderName,
+        'status': 'Pending',
+      });
+      return true;
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error sending connection invitation: $e');
+      rethrow;
+    }
+  }
+
+  Future<bool> sendCustomerConnectionInvitation({
+    required String customerEmail,
+    required String customerName,
+  }) async {
+    try {
+      final user = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+      if (user == null) throw Exception('Not logged in');
+
+      // Check if already invited or connected
+      final existingInvite = await _supabase
+          .from('customer_invitations')
+          .select('id')
+          .eq('builder_id', user.id)
+          .eq('customer_email', customerEmail)
+          .eq('status', 'Pending')
+          .maybeSingle();
+
+      if (existingInvite != null) {
+        throw Exception('An invitation has already been sent to this email.');
+      }
+
+      await _supabase.from('customer_invitations').insert({
+        'builder_id': user.id,
+        'customer_email': customerEmail,
+        'customer_name': customerName,
+        'status': 'Pending',
+      });
+      return true;
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error sending customer connection invitation: $e');
+      rethrow;
+    }
+  }
+
+  Future<bool> respondToConnectionInvitation({
+    required String invitationId,
+    required bool accept,
+  }) async {
+    try {
+      // 1. Find the invitation - check both tables
+      Map<String, dynamic>? inv;
+      var isBuilderInvitation = true;
+
+      inv = await _supabase
+          .from('builder_invitations')
+          .select()
+          .eq('id', invitationId)
+          .maybeSingle();
+
+      if (inv == null) {
+        inv = await _supabase
+            .from('customer_invitations')
+            .select()
+            .eq('id', invitationId)
+            .maybeSingle();
+        isBuilderInvitation = false;
+      }
+
+      if (inv == null) throw Exception('Invitation not found in either table');
+
+      // 2. Determine customer and builder IDs
+      // builder_invitations: customer sends to builder (customer is sender)
+      // customer_invitations: builder sends to customer (builder is sender)
+      final customerId = isBuilderInvitation
+          ? inv['customer_id']                       // customer is sender
+          : _supabase.auth.currentUser?.id;          // current user is customer (receiver)
+      final builderId = isBuilderInvitation
+          ? _supabase.auth.currentUser?.id           // current user is builder (receiver)
+          : inv['builder_id'];                       // builder is sender
+
+      if (customerId == null || builderId == null) {
+        throw Exception('Could not resolve IDs');
+      }
+
+      final newStatus = accept ? 'Accepted' : 'Declined';
+      
+      // 3. Update the status and updated_at timestamp so 24-hour auto-clearing starts
+      final nowStr = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
+      if (isBuilderInvitation) {
+        await _supabase.from('builder_invitations')
+            .update({
+              'status': newStatus,
+              'updated_at': nowStr,
+            })
+            .eq('id', invitationId);
+      } else {
+        await _supabase.from('customer_invitations')
+            .update({
+              'status': newStatus,
+              'updated_at': nowStr,
+            })
+            .eq('id', invitationId);
+      }
+
+      // 4. If accepted, create the assignment
+      if (accept) {
+        await _supabase.from('user_builder_assignments').upsert({
+          'customer_id': customerId,
+          'builder_id': builderId,
+          'report_id': null,
+          'assigned_at': DateTime.now().toIso8601String(),
+          'is_active': true,
+          'active_for_customer': true,
+          'active_for_builder': true,
+        }, onConflict: 'customer_id,builder_id,report_id');
+      }
+
+      // 5. Notify the inviter via email and in-app notification
+      try {
+        final inviterEmail = isBuilderInvitation ? inv['customer_email'] : inv['builder_email'];
+        final currentUser = _supabase.auth.currentUser;
+        final responderFirstName = currentUser?.userMetadata?['first_name'] ?? '';
+        final responderLastName = currentUser?.userMetadata?['last_name'] ?? '';
+        final responderName = '$responderFirstName $responderLastName'.trim();
+        final responderRole = isBuilderInvitation ? 'Builder' : 'Customer';
+
+        if (inviterEmail != null && inviterEmail.isNotEmpty) {
+          NotificationService.notifyInviteResponse(
+            inviterEmail: inviterEmail,
+            responderName: responderName.isNotEmpty ? responderName : 'A user',
+            responderRole: responderRole,
+            accepted: accept,
+          );
+        }
+      } catch (e) {
+        debugPrint('Error notifying inviter of response: $e');
+      }
+
+      return true;
+    } catch (e) {
+      debugPrint('Error responding to connection invitation: $e');
+      return false;
+    }
+  }
+

+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+import 'package:shimmer/shimmer.dart';
+import 'package:snagninja/utils/sizes.dart';
+
+class BuilderDashboardShimmer extends StatelessWidget {
+  const BuilderDashboardShimmer({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    AppSizes.init(context);
+    final shimmerColor = Colors.grey[300]!;
+    final highlightColor = Colors.grey[100]!;
+
+    return SingleChildScrollView(
+      padding: AppSizes.screenPadding.copyWith(bottom: 100),
+      child: Column(
+        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+        children: [
+          SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace),
+
+          // Stats Grid Shimmer
+          GridView.count(
+            crossAxisCount: AppSizes.screenWidth < 600 ? 2 : 4,
+            shrinkWrap: true,
+            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
+            childAspectRatio: AppSizes.screenWidth < 600 ? 1.5 : 1.2,
+            crossAxisSpacing: AppSizes.mediumSpace,
+            mainAxisSpacing: AppSizes.mediumSpace,
+            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
+            children: List.generate(
+              4,
+              (index) => _buildStatShimmer(shimmerColor, highlightColor),
+            ),
+          ),
+
+          SizedBox(height: AppSizes.largeSpace),
+
+          // Active Projects Title Shimmer
+          Shimmer.fromColors(
+            baseColor: shimmerColor,
+            highlightColor: highlightColor,
+            child: Container(
+              width: 150,
+              height: 24,
+              decoration: BoxDecoration(
+                color: Colors.white,
+                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
+              ),
+            ),
+          ),
+
+          SizedBox(height: AppSizes.smallSpace),
+
+          // Job Cards Shimmer
+          ListView.builder(
+            shrinkWrap: true,
+            physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
+            itemCount: 3,
+            itemBuilder:
+                (context, index) =>
+                    _buildJobCardShimmer(shimmerColor, highlightColor),
+          ),
+        ],
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildStatShimmer(Color baseColor, Color highlightColor) {
+    return Shimmer.fromColors(
+      baseColor: baseColor,
+      highlightColor: highlightColor,
+      child: Container(
+        decoration: BoxDecoration(
+          color: Colors.white,
+          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppSizes.containerRadius),
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+
+  Widget _buildJobCardShimmer(Color baseColor, Color highlightColor) {
+    return Padding(
+      padding: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AppSizes.mediumSpace),
+      child: Shimmer.fromColors(
+        baseColor: baseColor,
+        highlightColor: highlightColor,
+        child: Container(
+          height: 120,
+          decoration: BoxDecoration(
+            color: Colors.white,
+            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(AppSizes.containerRadius),
+          ),
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}

+  /// Show an error snackbar using a pre-captured [ScaffoldMessengerState].
+  /// Use this when showing a snackbar after an `await` to avoid
+  /// `use_build_context_synchronously` warnings.
+  static void showErrorViaMessenger({
+    required ScaffoldMessengerState messenger,
+    required String message,
+    Duration duration = const Duration(seconds: 3),
+  }) {
+    messenger.showSnackBar(
+      _buildSnackBar(
+        message: message,
+        leadingIcon: _redCircleIcon(),
+        duration: duration,
+      ),
+    );
+  }
 
+  /// Show a success snackbar using a pre-captured [ScaffoldMessengerState].
+  static void showSuccessViaMessenger({
+    required ScaffoldMessengerState messenger,
+    required String message,
+    Duration duration = const Duration(seconds: 3),
+  }) {
+    messenger.showSnackBar(
+      _buildSnackBar(
+        message: message,
+        leadingIcon: _greenCircleIcon(),
+        duration: duration,
+      ),
+    );
+  }
 
+  static SnackBar _buildSnackBar({
+    required String message,
+    required Widget leadingIcon,
+    Duration duration = const Duration(seconds: 3),
+  }) {
+    return SnackBar(
+      content: Row(
+        children: [
+          leadingIcon,
+          const SizedBox(width: 12),
+          Expanded(
+            child: Text(
+              message,
+              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.black, fontSize: 14),
+            ),
+          ),
+        ],
+      ),
+      duration: duration,
+      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
+      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
+      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20.0, vertical: 24.0),
+      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8.0)),
+      elevation: 4.0,
+      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
+    );
+  }
+
+  static Widget _redCircleIcon() => Container(
+        width: 24,
+        height: 24,
+        decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.red, shape: BoxShape.circle),
+        child: const Icon(Icons.error_outline, color: Colors.white, size: 16),
+      );
+
+  static Widget _greenCircleIcon() => Container(
+        width: 24,
+        height: 24,
+        decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: Colors.green, shape: BoxShape.circle),
+        child: const Icon(Icons.check, color: Colors.white, size: 16),
+      );
+}

## lib/widgets/top_notification_banner.dart

Provenance: scanner (filesystem metadata and bounded content acquisition)

````
# File: lib/widgets/top_notification_banner.dart

## Changes (+228/-0)

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,228 @@
+import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
+
+/// Floating dropdown banner displayed from the top of the screen.
+class TopNotificationBanner {
+  static OverlayEntry? _currentEntry;
+
+  /// Display a sleek top notification banner.
+  static void show({
+    required BuildContext context,
+    required String title,
+    required String message,
+    IconData icon = Icons.notifications_active_rounded,
+    Color backgroundColor = const Color(0xFF1A365D),
+    Color accentColor = const Color(0xFF3182CE),
+    Duration duration = const Duration(seconds: 4),
+    VoidCallback? onTap,
+  }) {
+    _currentEntry?.remove();
+    _currentEntry = null;
+
+    final overlay = Overlay.maybeOf(context);
+    if (overlay == null) return;
+
+    late OverlayEntry entry;
+    entry = OverlayEntry(
+      builder: (context) => _TopBannerWidget(
+        title: title,
+        message: message,
+        icon: icon,
+        backgroundColor: backgroundColor,
+        accentColor: accentColor,
+        duration: duration,
+        onTap: onTap,
+        onDismiss: () {
+          entry.remove();
+          if (_currentEntry == entry) {
+            _currentEntry = null;
+          }
+        },
+      ),
+    );
+
+    _currentEntry = entry;
+    overlay.insert(entry);
+  }
+}
+
+class _TopBannerWidget extends StatefulWidget {
+  final String title;
+  final String message;
+  final IconData icon;
+  final Color backgroundColor;
+  final Color accentColor;
+  final Duration duration;
+  final VoidCallback? onTap;
+  final VoidCallback onDismiss;
+
+  const _TopBannerWidget({
+    required this.title,
+    required this.message,
+    required this.icon,
+    required this.backgroundColor,
+    required this.accentColor,
+    required this.duration,
+    this.onTap,
+    required this.onDismiss,
+  });
+
+  @override
+  State<_TopBannerWidget> createState() => _TopBannerWidgetState();
+}
+
+class _TopBannerWidgetState extends State<_TopBannerWidget>
+    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
+  late final AnimationController _controller;
+  late final Animation<Offset> _offsetAnimation;
+  late final Animation<double> _fadeAnimation;
+
+  @override
+  void initState() {
+    super.initState();
+    _controller = AnimationController(
+      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 350),
+      vsync: this,
+    );
+
+    _offsetAnimation = Tween<Offset>(
+      begin: const Offset(0.0, -1.0),
+      end: Offset.zero,
+    ).animate(CurvedAnimation(
+      parent: _controller,
+      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
+    ));
+
+    _fadeAnimation = CurvedAnimation(
+      parent: _controller,
+      curve: Curves.easeIn,
+    );
+
+    _controller.forward();
+
+    // Auto-dismiss timer
+    Future.delayed(widget.duration, () {
+      if (mounted) {
+        _dismiss();
+      }
+    });
+  }
+
+  void _dismiss() async {
+    await _controller.reverse();
+    widget.onDismiss();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void dispose() {
+    _controller.dispose();
+    super.dispose();
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    final topPadding = MediaQuery.of(context).padding.top + 12;
+
+    return Positioned(
+      top: 0,
+      left: 0,
+      right: 0,
+      child: SlideTransition(
+        position: _offsetAnimation,
+        child: FadeTransition(
+          opacity: _fadeAnimation,
+          child: Padding(
+            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: topPadding, left: 16, right: 16),
+            child: Material(
+              color: Colors.transparent,
+              child: GestureDetector(
+                onTap: () {
+                  widget.onTap?.call();
+                  _dismiss();
+                },
+                child: Container(
+                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 14),
+                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
+                    color: widget.backgroundColor,
+                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
+                    boxShadow: [
+                      BoxShadow(
+                        color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
+                        blurRadius: 16,
+                        offset: const Offset(0, 6),
+                      ),
+                    ],
+                    border: Border.all(
+                      color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
+                      width: 1,
+                    ),
+                  ),
+                  child: Row(
+                    children: [
+                      Container(
+                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
+                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
+                          color: widget.accentColor.withValues(alpha: 0.2),
+                          shape: BoxShape.circle,
+                        ),
+                        child: Icon(
+                          widget.icon,
+                          color: Colors.white,
+                          size: 22,
+                        ),
+                      ),
+                      const SizedBox(width: 14),
+                      Expanded(
+                        child: Column(
+                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
+                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
+                          children: [
+                            Text(
+                              widget.title,
+                              style: const TextStyle(
+                                color: Colors.white,
+                                fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
+                                fontSize: 14,
+                              ),
+                              maxLines: 1,
+                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
+                            ),
+                            const SizedBox(height: 3),
+                            Text(
+                              widget.message,
+                              style: TextStyle(
+                                color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.9),
+                                fontSize: 12,
+                              ),
+                              maxLines: 2,
+                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
+                            ),
+                          ],
+                        ),
+                      ),
+                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
+                      GestureDetector(
+                        onTap: _dismiss,
+                        child: Container(
+                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
+                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
+                            color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.15),
+                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
+                          ),
+                          child: const Icon(
+                            Icons.close,
+                            color: Colors.white,
+                            size: 16,
+                          ),
+                        ),
+                      ),
+                    ],
+                  ),
+                ),
+              ),
+            ),
+          ),
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}

## supabase/functions/send-invite-email/index.ts

# File: supabase/functions/send-invite-email/index.ts

## Changes (+152/-0)

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,152 @@
+// @ts-nocheck
+import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";
+import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";
+
+const RESEND_API_KEY = Deno.env.get("RESEND_API_KEY");
+const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL");
+const SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
+
+const FROM_EMAIL = "SnagNinja <no-reply@notify.snagninja.com>";
+
+// TODO: Replace with real Play Store URL when SnagNinja is published
+const DEFAULT_PLAY_STORE_URL = "https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.snagninja.app";
+const DEFAULT_APP_DEEP_LINK  = "snagninja://invites/received";
+
+const corsHeaders = {
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
+};
+
+serve(async (req) => {
+  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
+    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const body = await req.json();
+    // sender_name arrives already formatted as "Name (Builder)" or "Name (Customer)" from Flutter
+    const { to, sender_name, exists, role, app_link, play_store_url } = body;
+
+    if (!to || !sender_name || role === undefined) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing required fields: to, sender_name, role" }),
+        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+      );
+    }
+
+    // role = recipient's role (e.g. "Customer" means the email goes to a customer)
+    const recipientRole = role;
+    const appDeepLink   = app_link       || DEFAULT_APP_DEEP_LINK;
+    const playStoreUrl  = play_store_url || DEFAULT_PLAY_STORE_URL;
+
+    // 1. Insert In-App Notification using Service Role Key (bypasses RLS)
+    if (SUPABASE_URL && SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY) {
+      try {
+        const supabaseAdmin = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY);
+
+        const { data: profile } = await supabaseAdmin
+          .from("user_profiles")
+          .select("id")
+          .ilike("email", to)
+          .maybeSingle();
+
+        const recipientUserId = profile?.id;
+
+        if (recipientUserId) {
+          await supabaseAdmin.from("notifications").insert({
+            user_id: recipientUserId,
+            title: "🤝 New Connection Invitation",
+            message: `${sender_name} has sent you a connection invitation on SnagNinja. Open the app to accept or decline.`,
+            report_id: null,
+            is_read: false,
+            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+          });
+          console.log(`In-app invitation notification inserted for ${to} (user_id: ${recipientUserId})`);
+        }
+      } catch (e) {
+        console.error("Failed to insert in-app notification via Edge Function:", e);
+      }
+    }
+
+    // 2. Build Email Content
+    const subject = exists
+      ? `New Connection Invitation from ${sender_name}`
+      : `Invitation to join SnagNinja from ${sender_name}`;
+
+    const htmlContent = `
+      <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+        <h2 style="color: #1a365d; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">🤝 Connection Invitation</h2>
+        <p>Hello,</p>
+        <p><strong>${sender_name}</strong> wants to connect with you on SnagNinja as a <strong>${recipientRole}</strong>.</p>
+
+        ${exists
+          ? `<p style="background: #ebf8ff; padding: 12px 16px; border-radius: 8px; color: #2b6cb0; font-size: 14px; margin: 16px 0;">
+               ℹ️ You already have a SnagNinja account. Open the app to respond.
+             </p>`
+          : `<p style="background: #f0fff4; padding: 12px 16px; border-radius: 8px; color: #276749; font-size: 14px; margin: 16px 0;">
+               🎉 You're invited to join SnagNinja! Download the app to get started.
+             </p>`
+        }
+
+        <div style="background: #f7fafc; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding: 20px; border-radius: 8px; margin: 24px 0; text-align: center;">
+          <p style="margin-top: 0; font-size: 15px; color: #2d3748;">Open the SnagNinja app to respond to this invitation:</p>
+          <div style="margin: 16px 0;">
+            <a href="${appDeepLink}?action=accept"
+               style="background-color: #38a169; color: white; padding: 12px 22px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block; margin: 4px;">
+              ✅ Accept
+            </a>
+            <a href="${appDeepLink}?action=decline"
+               style="background-color: #e53e3e; color: white; padding: 12px 22px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block; margin: 4px;">
+              ❌ Decline
+            </a>
+          </div>
+          <p style="color: #718096; font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 0;">Buttons open the SnagNinja app on the Invitations screen.</p>
+        </div>
+
+        <div style="background: #ebf8ff; border-left: 4px solid #3182ce; padding: 16px; border-radius: 4px; margin: 20px 0;">
+          <p style="margin: 0; font-size: 14px; color: #2b6cb0;">
+            📲 <strong>Don't have the SnagNinja app installed?</strong><br/>
+            <a href="${playStoreUrl}" style="color: #2b6cb0; font-weight: bold; text-decoration: underline;">Click here to download SnagNinja on Google Play Store</a>
+            — then open the app and go to the <strong>Invitations</strong> tab.
+          </p>
+        </div>
+
+        <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid #e2e8f0; margin: 24px 0;">
+        <p style="color: #999; font-size: 12px; text-align: center;">This is an automated notification from SnagNinja.</p>
+      </div>
+    `;
+
+    // 3. Send Email via Resend API
+    if (!RESEND_API_KEY) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({ error: "RESEND_API_KEY environment variable is not configured" }),
+        { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+      );
+    }
+
+    const res = await fetch("https://api.resend.com/emails", {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${RESEND_API_KEY}`,
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        from: FROM_EMAIL,
+        to: [to],
+        subject: subject,
+        html: htmlContent,
+      }),
+    });
+
+    const data = await res.json();
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
+      status: res.status,
+      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+    });
+  } catch (error: any) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }),
+      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+    );
+  }
+});
```
````

@@ -0,0 +1,335 @@
+// @ts-nocheck
+import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";
+import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";
+
+const RESEND_API_KEY = Deno.env.get("RESEND_API_KEY");
+const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL");
+const SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
+
+const FROM_EMAIL = "SnagNinja <no-reply@notify.snagninja.com>";
+
+// TODO: Replace with real Play Store URL when SnagNinja is published
+const PLAY_STORE_URL = "https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.snagninja.app";
+
+const corsHeaders = {
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
+};
+
+function playStoreBlock(playStoreUrl: string): string {
+  return `
+    <div style="background: #ebf8ff; border-left: 4px solid #3182ce; padding: 14px; border-radius: 4px; margin: 20px 0;">
+      <p style="margin: 0; font-size: 13px; color: #2b6cb0;">
+        📲 <strong>App not installed?</strong>
+        <a href="${playStoreUrl}" style="color: #2b6cb0; font-weight: bold; text-decoration: underline;">Click here to download SnagNinja on Google Play Store</a>.
+      </p>
+    </div>`;
+}
+
+function emailFooter(): string {
+  return `
+    <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid #e2e8f0; margin: 24px 0;">
+    <p style="color: #999; font-size: 12px; text-align: center;">This is an automated notification from SnagNinja.</p>`;
+}
+
+function buildEmailContent(
+  event_type: string,
+  params: Record<string, string>,
+  appDeepLink: string,
+  playStoreUrl: string,
+): { subject: string; html: string } {
+  const { sender_name, recipient_name, report_title, property_address } = params;
+
+  const reportCard = `
+    <div style="background: #ebf8ff; border-left: 4px solid #3182ce; padding: 16px; border-radius: 4px; margin: 20px 0;">
+      <p style="margin: 0;"><strong>Report:</strong> ${report_title}</p>
+      <p style="margin: 8px 0 0;"><strong>Property:</strong> ${property_address}</p>
+    </div>`;
+
+  const reportCardGreen = `
+    <div style="background: #f0fff4; border-left: 4px solid #38a169; padding: 16px; border-radius: 4px; margin: 20px 0;">
+      <p style="margin: 0;"><strong>Report:</strong> ${report_title}</p>
+      <p style="margin: 8px 0 0;"><strong>Property:</strong> ${property_address}</p>
+    </div>`;
+
+  const reportCardOrange = `
+    <div style="background: #fffaf0; border-left: 4px solid #ed8936; padding: 16px; border-radius: 4px; margin: 20px 0;">
+      <p style="margin: 0;"><strong>Report:</strong> ${report_title}</p>
+      <p style="margin: 8px 0 0;"><strong>Property:</strong> ${property_address}</p>
+    </div>`;
+
+  switch (event_type) {
+
+    // ── Customer receives: builder submitted report for review ───────────────
+    case "report_submitted_for_review":
+      return {
+        subject: `Report Ready for Your Review: ${report_title}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #1a365d; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">Report Submitted for Your Review</h2>
+            <p>Hello <strong>${recipient_name ?? "there"}</strong>,</p>
+            <p>Your builder <strong>${sender_name}</strong> has addressed the defects in your snagging report and submitted it for your review.</p>
+            ${reportCard}
+            <div style="background: #f7fafc; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding: 18px; border-radius: 8px; margin: 24px 0; text-align: center;">
+              <p style="margin-top: 0; font-size: 15px; color: #2d3748;">Open the SnagNinja app to review and sign off:</p>
+              <div style="margin: 14px 0;">
+                <a href="${appDeepLink}" style="background-color: #2b6cb0; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block;">View Report in App</a>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+            ${playStoreBlock(playStoreUrl)}
+            ${emailFooter()}
+          </div>`,
+      };
+
+    // ── Builder receives: customer signed off report ─────────────────────────
+    case "report_completed":
+      return {
+        subject: `Great Work! Report Signed Off: ${report_title}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #276749; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">🎉 Report Successfully Completed!</h2>
+            <p>Hello <strong>${recipient_name ?? "there"}</strong>,</p>
+            <p>Fantastic news! <strong>${sender_name}</strong> has reviewed your work and signed off the snagging report as <strong>completed</strong>.</p>
+            ${reportCardGreen}
+            <div style="background: #f7fafc; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding: 18px; border-radius: 8px; margin: 24px 0; text-align: center;">
+              <div style="margin: 14px 0;">
+                <a href="${appDeepLink}" style="background-color: #38a169; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block;">View Completed Report</a>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+            ${playStoreBlock(playStoreUrl)}
+            ${emailFooter()}
+          </div>`,
+      };
+
+    // ── Builder receives: customer shared a new report ───────────────────────
+    case "report_shared":
+      return {
+        subject: `New Snagging Report Shared With You: ${report_title}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #1a365d; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">📋 New Snagging Report Shared With You</h2>
+            <p>Hello <strong>${recipient_name ?? "there"}</strong>,</p>
+            <p><strong>${sender_name}</strong> has shared a new snagging report with you to review and address.</p>
+            ${reportCard}
+            <div style="background: #f7fafc; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding: 18px; border-radius: 8px; margin: 24px 0; text-align: center;">
+              <p style="margin-top: 0; font-size: 15px; color: #2d3748;">Open the SnagNinja app to view the defects:</p>
+              <div style="margin: 14px 0;">
+                <a href="${appDeepLink}" style="background-color: #2b6cb0; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block;">Open Report in App</a>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+            ${playStoreBlock(playStoreUrl)}
+            ${emailFooter()}
+          </div>`,
+      };
+
+    // ── Builder receives: customer added a new issue ─────────────────────────
+    case "new_issue_added":
+      return {
+        subject: `New Issue Added to Report: ${report_title}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #c05621; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">🔔 New Issue Added to Report</h2>
+            <p>Hello <strong>${recipient_name ?? "there"}</strong>,</p>
+            <p><strong>${sender_name}</strong> has added a new issue to the snagging report. The report is now back in <strong>Active</strong> status — please review and address the new issue.</p>
+            ${reportCardOrange}
+            <div style="background: #f7fafc; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding: 18px; border-radius: 8px; margin: 24px 0; text-align: center;">
+              <p style="margin-top: 0; font-size: 15px; color: #2d3748;">Open the SnagNinja app to view the new issue:</p>
+              <div style="margin: 14px 0;">
+                <a href="${appDeepLink}" style="background-color: #ed8936; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block;">View New Issue in App</a>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+            ${playStoreBlock(playStoreUrl)}
+            ${emailFooter()}
+          </div>`,
+      };
+
+    // ── Legacy: report assigned (PDF share) ─────────────────────────────────
+    case "report_assigned":
+      return {
+        subject: `New Report Assigned to You: ${report_title}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #1a365d; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">New Report Assigned</h2>
+            <p>Hello <strong>${recipient_name ?? "there"}</strong>,</p>
+            <p><strong>${sender_name}</strong> has assigned you a new snagging report to review and address.</p>
+            ${reportCard}
+            <div style="background: #f7fafc; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; padding: 18px; border-radius: 8px; margin: 24px 0; text-align: center;">
+              <p style="margin-top: 0; font-size: 15px; color: #2d3748;">Open the SnagNinja app to view the defects:</p>
+              <div style="margin: 14px 0;">
+                <a href="${appDeepLink}" style="background-color: #2b6cb0; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block;">Open Assigned Report</a>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+            ${playStoreBlock(playStoreUrl)}
+            ${emailFooter()}
+          </div>`,
+      };
+
+    // ── Invite accepted ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    case "invite_accepted":
+      return {
+        subject: `✅ Invitation Accepted: ${sender_name}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #276749; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">✅ Connection Invitation Accepted</h2>
+            <p>Hello,</p>
+            <p><strong>${sender_name}</strong> has accepted your connection invitation on SnagNinja.</p>
+            <p>You can now collaborate on property snagging reports together in your <strong>SnagNinja app</strong>.</p>
+            ${emailFooter()}
+          </div>`,
+      };
+
+    // ── Invite declined ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    case "invite_declined":
+      return {
+        subject: `❌ Invitation Declined: ${sender_name}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #c53030; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">❌ Connection Invitation Declined</h2>
+            <p>Hello,</p>
+            <p><strong>${sender_name}</strong> has declined your connection invitation on SnagNinja.</p>
+            ${emailFooter()}
+          </div>`,
+      };
+
+    default:
+      return {
+        subject: "SnagNinja Notification",
+        html: `<p>You have a new notification from SnagNinja.</p>`,
+      };
+  }
+}
+
+serve(async (req: Request) => {
+  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
+    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const body = await req.json();
+    const {
+      to,
+      event_type,
+      sender_name,
+      recipient_name,
+      report_title,
+      property_address,
+      report_id,
+      app_link,
+      play_store_url,
+    } = body;
+
+    if (!to || !event_type) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing required fields: to, event_type" }),
+        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+      );
+    }
+
+    const playStoreUrl = play_store_url || PLAY_STORE_URL;
+    const appDeepLink  = app_link || (report_id ? `snagninja://report/${report_id}` : "snagninja://reports");
+
+    // 1. Insert In-App Notification using Service Role Key (bypasses RLS)
+    if (SUPABASE_URL && SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY) {
+      try {
+        const supabaseAdmin = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY);
+
+        const { data: profile } = await supabaseAdmin
+          .from("user_profiles")
+          .select("id")
+          .ilike("email", to)
+          .maybeSingle();
+
+        const recipientUserId = profile?.id;
+
+        let notifTitle = "Notification";
+        let notifMsg   = "";
+
+        switch (event_type) {
+          case "report_submitted_for_review":
+            notifTitle = "📋 Report Ready for Review";
+            notifMsg = `${sender_name} has submitted "${report_title ?? "Snagging Report"}" for your review. Please open the app to sign off.`;
+            break;
+          case "report_completed":
+            notifTitle = "🎉 Report Signed Off & Completed!";
+            notifMsg = `${sender_name} has signed off "${report_title ?? "Snagging Report"}". Excellent work! The snagging report is now closed. Thank you! 🙌`;
+            break;
+          case "report_shared":
+            notifTitle = "📋 New Snagging Report Shared";
+            notifMsg = `${sender_name} has shared "${report_title ?? "a snagging report"}" with you.`;
+            break;
+          case "new_issue_added":
+            notifTitle = "🔔 New Issue Added";
+            notifMsg = `${sender_name} has added a new issue to "${report_title ?? "Snagging Report"}". The report is back in Active status.`;
+            break;
+          case "report_assigned":
+            notifTitle = "📋 New Report Assigned";
+            notifMsg = `${sender_name} has assigned you a new snagging report: "${report_title ?? "Snagging Report"}".`;
+            break;
+          case "invite_accepted":
+            notifTitle = "✅ Invitation Accepted";
+            notifMsg = `${sender_name} has accepted your connection invitation on SnagNinja.`;
+            break;
+          case "invite_declined":
+            notifTitle = "❌ Invitation Declined";
+            notifMsg = `${sender_name} has declined your connection invitation.`;
+            break;
+        }
+
+        if (recipientUserId) {
+          await supabaseAdmin.from("notifications").insert({
+            user_id: recipientUserId,
+            title: notifTitle,
+            message: notifMsg,
+            report_id: report_id ?? null,
+            is_read: false,
+            created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+          });
+          console.log(`In-app notification (${event_type}) inserted for ${to} (user_id: ${recipientUserId})`);
+        }
+      } catch (e) {
+        console.error("Failed to insert in-app notification via Edge Function:", e);
+      }
+    }
+
+    // 2. Send Email via Resend API
+    if (!RESEND_API_KEY) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({ error: "RESEND_API_KEY not configured" }),
+        { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+      );
+    }
+
+    const { subject, html } = buildEmailContent(
+      event_type,
+      {
+        sender_name: sender_name ?? "SnagNinja",
+        recipient_name: recipient_name ?? "",
+        report_title: report_title ?? "Snagging Report",
+        property_address: property_address ?? "",
+        report_id: report_id ?? "",
+      },
+      appDeepLink,
+      playStoreUrl,
+    );
+
+    const res = await fetch("https://api.resend.com/emails", {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${RESEND_API_KEY}`,
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({ from: FROM_EMAIL, to: [to], subject, html }),
+    });
+
+    const data = await res.json();
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
+      status: res.status,
+      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+    });
+  } catch (error: any) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }),
+      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+    );
+  }
+});
```
````

+// @ts-nocheck
+import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts";
+
+const RESEND_API_KEY = Deno.env.get("RESEND_API_KEY");
+const FROM_EMAIL = "SnagNinja <no-reply@notify.snagninja.com>";
+
+const corsHeaders = {
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
+  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
+};
+
+serve(async (req) => {
+  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") {
+    return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const { to, report_url, report_title, property_address } = await req.json();
+
+    if (!to || !report_url) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({ error: "Missing required fields: to, report_url" }),
+        { status: 400, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+      );
+    }
+
+    if (!RESEND_API_KEY) {
+      return new Response(
+        JSON.stringify({ error: "RESEND_API_KEY environment variable is not configured on Supabase" }),
+        { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+      );
+    }
+
+    const res = await fetch("https://api.resend.com/emails", {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {
+        "Content-Type": "application/json",
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${RESEND_API_KEY}`,
+      },
+      body: JSON.stringify({
+        from: FROM_EMAIL,
+        to: [to],
+        subject: `New Report Assigned: ${report_title}`,
+        html: `
+          <div style="font-family: sans-serif; padding: 24px; color: #333; max-width: 620px; margin: 0 auto; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 12px;">
+            <h2 style="color: #1a365d; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 8px;">New Report Assigned</h2>
+            <p>Hello,</p>
+            <p>You have been assigned to a new report for the property at <strong>${property_address}</strong>.</p>
+            <p><strong>Report:</strong> ${report_title}</p>
+            <div style="margin: 24px 0; text-align: center;">
+              <a href="${report_url}" style="background-color: #2b6cb0; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: bold; display: inline-block;">Download Report PDF</a>
+            </div>
+            <p style="color: #666; font-size: 14px;">If you don't have the SnagNinja app installed, you can view and download the report using the button above.</p>
+            <hr style="border: 0; border-top: 1px solid #e2e8f0; margin: 24px 0;">
+            <p style="color: #999; font-size: 12px; text-align: center;">This is an automated notification from SnagNinja.</p>
+          </div>
+        `,
+      }),
+    });
+
+    const data = await res.json();
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
+      status: res.status,
+      headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+    });
+  } catch (error: any) {
+    return new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }),
+      { status: 500, headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json" } }
+    );
+  }
+});
